# ROMS cppdefs.h — 문서와 코드 차이 (10-14 대조)

출처: [biology 대조 보고서](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-biology-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론), [build-options-history 대조 보고서](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론).
최종 결론은 각 보고서 끝의 [biology 검증 기록](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-biology-vs-code.md#전수-검증-기록-10-14)과 [build-options-history 검증 기록](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md#전수-검증-기록-10-14)을 따른다.

문서 판: ROMS 위키 wikitext, 2026-05-03 받음, 쪽별 revid는 [manifest.json](../raw/manuals/wiki/manifest.json). 코드 판: 원본 저장소 HEAD `57aecf58`, 2026-09-18, roms-4.2 이후 4.3 개발.

Set bottom flux (Tunits m/s) of passive tracers at RHO-point,`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_btflux.h:106 `      ELSE`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_btflux.h:109 `            btflux(i,j,itrc)=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:85 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:86 `#  if defined ANA_BIOLOGY && defined BIOLOGY`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:197 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:198 `#  if defined ANA_SEDIMENT && (defined SEDIMENT \|\| defined BBL_MODEL)`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:176 `** ANA_BPFLUX              if analytical bottom passive tracers fluxes       **`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:2 `#ifdef NONLINEAR`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:193 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:536 `#  if defined BIOLOGY \|\| defined SEDIMENT \|\| defined T_PASSIVE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:537 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:538 `!  Set kinematic surface and bottom passive tracer fluxes (T m/s).`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:539 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:540 `      DO itrc=NAT+1,NT(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:551 `#   ifdef ANA_BPFLUX`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:552 `        CALL ana_btflux (ng, tile, iNLM, itrc)` | B 문서 오기 | ANA_BPFLUX는 NONLINEAR·SOLVE3D의 수동 추적자 플럭스 경로에서 ana_btflux를 선택한다. BIOLOGY, SEDIMENT, T_PASSIVE 중 하나가 필요하다. BIOLOGY와 SEDIMENT를 동시에 요구하지 않는다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-0336 | ANA_FSOBC required | Options.wiki:188 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;OBC&#93;&#93;</code> | roms-3.3:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:739 `# ifdef OBC`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:752 `#  ifdef ANA_FSOBC`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:753 `      CALL ana_fsobc (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>`e8719fb7 2011-09-23 src:ticket:519`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:117 `# ifdef ANA_FSOBC`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:118 `#  include <ana_fsobc.h>`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/ana_fsobc.h:178 `      IF (LBC(ieast,isFsur,ng)%acquire.and.                             &`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/ana_fsobc.h:179 `     &    DOMAIN(ng)%Eastern_Edge(tile)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:357 `      TYPE T_LBC`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:360 `        logical :: Chapman_explicit`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:361 `        logical :: Chapman_implicit`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:362 `        logical :: clamped`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:363 `        logical :: closed`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:364 `        logical :: Flather`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:365 `        logical :: gradient`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:366 `        logical :: mixed`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:368 `        logical :: nudging`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:369 `        logical :: periodic`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:370 `        logical :: radiation`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:371 `        logical :: reduced`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:375 `      TYPE (T_LBC), allocatable :: LBC(:,:,:)`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:183 `** ANA_FSOBC               if analytical free-surface boundary conditions    **` | A 판 차이 | 과거 ANA_FSOBC 호출은 OBC 분기 안에 있었다. 2011년에 OBC 전처리 선택이 삭제됐다. 현재 경계 취득 조건은 LBC에서 읽는다.<br>git 판정 커밋: `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |
| build-options-history:RBH-0346 | ANA_HUMIDITY required | Options.wiki:202 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;ALBEDO&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;BULK_FLUXES&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;ECOSIM&#93;&#93;</code> | ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:125 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:126 `#  if defined ANA_HUMIDITY && \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:127 `    (defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:128 `    (defined ANA_SRFLUX  && defined ALBEDO) )`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:129 `#   include <ana_humid.h>`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:185 `** ANA_HUMIDITY            if analytical surface air humidity                **` | B 문서 오기 | analytical.F의 ANALYTICAL·SOLVE3D 분기에서 ANA_HUMIDITY를 검사한다. 추가 선택지는 BULK_FLUXES, ECOSIM, 또는 ANA_SRFLUX와 ALBEDO의 동시 정의이다. 문서에 열거된 세 옵션을 모두 요구하지 않는다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-0355 | ANA_M2CLIMA required | Options.wiki:215 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;M2CLIMATOLOGY&#93;&#93;</code> | roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:128 `# if defined ANA_M2CLIMA && defined M2CLIMATOLOGY`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:129 `#  include <ana_m2clima.h>`<br>`46f20897 2014-03-13 src:ticket:627`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:137 `# ifdef ANA_M2CLIMA`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:1313 `      IF (Lm2CLM(ng)) THEN`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:1314 `# ifdef ANA_M2CLIMA`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:1315 `        CALL ana_m2clima (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:530 `        logical, allocatable :: Lm2CLM(:)            ! 2D momentum`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:187 `** ANA_M2CLIMA             if analytical 2D momentum climatology             **`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:138 `#  include <ana_m2clima.h>`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:2 `#ifdef NONLINEAR` | A 판 차이 | ANA_M2CLIMA의 M2CLIMATOLOGY 필수 조건은 ROMS 3.6과 맞는다. 2014년 변경 이후 호출 여부는 Lm2CLM이 정한다. 현재 ana_m2clima 호출은 NONLINEAR에서 ANA_M2CLIMA가 정의되고 `Lm2CLM(ng)`가 참인 경우이다.<br>git 판정 커밋: `46f20897` (2014-03-13). |
| build-options-history:RBH-0356 | ANA_M2CLIMA related | Options.wiki:216 <code>:'''related = ''' &#91;&#91;M2CLM_NUDGING&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;UV_TIDES&#93;&#93;</code> | roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:98 `# ifdef M2CLIMATOLOGY`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:99 `     &                  CLIMA(ng) % ubarclm,    CLIMA(ng) % vbarclm,    &`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:100 `#  ifdef M2CLM_NUDGING`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:101 `     &                  CLIMA(ng) % M2nudgcof,                          &`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:449 `#  if defined M2CLIMATOLOGY && defined ADD_M2OBC`<br>`46f20897 2014-03-13 src:ticket:627`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2244 `      IF (LnudgeM2CLM(ng)) THEN`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:537 `        logical, allocatable :: LnudgeM2CLM(:)       ! 2D momentum`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main2d.F:2 `#if defined NONLINEAR && !defined SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main2d.F:76 `# if defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:137 `# ifdef ANA_M2CLIMA`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:187 `** ANA_M2CLIMA             if analytical 2D momentum climatology             **`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:138 `#  include <ana_m2clima.h>` | A 판 차이 | 과거 M2CLM_NUDGING은 2차원 운동량 기후장을 사용했다. 2014년 변경 이후 이 선택은 LnudgeM2CLM이 맡는다.<br>git 판정 커밋: `46f20897` (2014-03-13). |

| build-options-history:RBH-0374 | ANA_MASK 설명 | Options.wiki:242 `:Option to set land/sea masking using the analytical set-up. It is used in few idealized test problems. | build-options-history:RBH-0386 | ANA_PERTURB 설명 | Options.wiki:260 `:Option to perturb tangent linear and adjoint state initial conditions at specified spatial (i,j,k) location. This is used to determine the correctness of the tangent linear and adjoint models by computing the inner product at the specified location. This inner product must be symmetric within roundoff (A - A<sup>T</sup> = 0).` | Master/roms_kernel.F:114 `#if defined AD_SENSITIVITY`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:117 `#elif defined AFT_EIGENMODES`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:119 `#elif defined FT_EIGENMODES`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:121 `#elif defined FORCING_SV`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:123 `#elif defined OPT_PERTURBATION`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:125 `#elif defined HESSIAN_SV`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:127 `#elif defined HESSIAN_FSV`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:129 `#elif defined OPT_OBSERVATIONS`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:131 `#elif defined STOCHASTIC_OPT`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:137 `#elif defined SO_SEMI`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:140 `#elif defined CORRELATION`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:142 `#elif defined INNER_PRODUCT \|\| defined SANITY_CHECK`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:143 `# include "pert_roms.h"`<br>ROMS/Drivers/pert_roms.h:19 `!  If each interior point is perturbed at one time,  the  resulting    !`<br>ROMS/Drivers/pert_roms.h:20 `!  tangent linear (T) and adjoint (A) M-by-N matrices yield:           !`<br>ROMS/Drivers/pert_roms.h:22 `!                T - tranpose(A) = 0    within round off               !`<br>ROMS/Drivers/pert_roms.h:24 `!  That is, their inner product give a symmetric matrix. Here, M is    !`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:195 `** ANA_PERTURB             if analytical perturbation of initial conditions  **`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:170 `# if defined ANA_PERTURB`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:171 `#  include <ana_perturb.h>`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL` | B 문서 오기 | 접선형 T와 수반 A의 전치 관계를 분명히 적는다. 문서의 A 기호를 정의 없이 반복하지 않는다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-0389 | ANA_PERTURB required | Options.wiki:263 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;SANITY_CHECK&#93;&#93;</code> | ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:439 `#if !defined ANA_PERTURB     && \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:440 `    (defined CORRELATION     \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:441 `     defined SANITY_CHECK    \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:442 `     defined R_SYMMETRY)`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:114 `#if defined AD_SENSITIVITY`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:117 `#elif defined AFT_EIGENMODES`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:119 `#elif defined FT_EIGENMODES`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:121 `#elif defined FORCING_SV`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:123 `#elif defined OPT_PERTURBATION`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:125 `#elif defined HESSIAN_SV`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:127 `#elif defined HESSIAN_FSV`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:129 `#elif defined OPT_OBSERVATIONS`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:131 `#elif defined STOCHASTIC_OPT`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:137 `#elif defined SO_SEMI`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:140 `#elif defined CORRELATION`<br>Master/roms_kernel.F:142 `#elif defined INNER_PRODUCT \|\| defined SANITY_CHECK`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:170 `# if defined ANA_PERTURB`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:443 `# define ANA_PERTURB`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:195 `** ANA_PERTURB             if analytical perturbation of initial conditions  **`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:171 `#  include <ana_perturb.h>` | B 문서 오기 | globaldefs.h는 ANA_PERTURB가 없고 CORRELATION, SANITY_CHECK, R_SYMMETRY 중 하나가 정의되면 ANA_PERTURB를 정의한다. analytical.F는 ANALYTICAL 분기 안에서 ANA_PERTURB가 정의되면 ana_perturb.h를 포함한다. SANITY_CHECK는 모든 사용의 필수 조건이 아니다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-0406 | ANA_SEDIMENT required | Options.wiki:286 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;SEDIMENT&#93;&#93;</code> | ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:197 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:198 `#  if defined ANA_SEDIMENT && (defined SEDIMENT \|\| defined BBL_MODEL)`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:199 `#   include <ana_sediment.h>`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:198 `** ANA_SEDIMENT            if analytical sediment initial fields             **` | B 문서 오기 | analytical.F의 ANALYTICAL·SOLVE3D 분기에서 ANA_SEDIMENT를 검사한다. | build-options-history:RBH-0432 | ANA_SSH required | Options.wiki:324 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;ZCLIMATOLOGY&#93;&#93;</code> | roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:197 `# if defined ANA_SSH && defined ZCLIMATOLOGY`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:198 `#  include <ana_ssh.h>`<br>`46f20897 2014-03-13 src:ticket:627`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:223 `# ifdef ANA_SSH`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:1298 `      IF (LsshCLM(ng)) THEN`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:1299 `# ifdef ANA_SSH`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:1300 `        CALL ana_ssh (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:529 `        logical, allocatable :: LsshCLM(:)           ! free-surface`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:205 `** ANA_SSH                 if analytical sea surface height                  **`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:224 `#  include <ana_ssh.h>`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_data.F:2 `#ifdef NONLINEAR` | A 판 차이 | ANA_SSH의 ZCLIMATOLOGY 필수 조건은 ROMS 3.6과 맞는다. 2014년 변경 이후 호출 여부는 LsshCLM이 정한다. 현재 ana_ssh 호출은 NONLINEAR에서 ANA_SSH가 정의되고 `LsshCLM(ng)`가 참인 경우이다.<br>git 판정 커밋: `46f20897` (2014-03-13). |

It also sets surface net heat flux sensitivity to sea surface temperature. Both fields are used for surface net heat flux correction. ` | roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/ana_sst.h:54 `     &                         sst, dqdt)`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/ana_sst.h:92 `          sst(i,j)=???`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Functionals/ana_sst.h:93 `          dqdt(i,j)=???`<br>`2bc0778d 2013-10-15 src:ticket:609`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:107 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:108 `#  if defined ANA_DQDSST && defined QCORRECTION`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:109 `#   include <ana_dqdsst.h>`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:234 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:235 `#  if defined ANA_SST && defined QCORRECTION`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:236 `#   include <ana_sst.h>`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_dqdsst.h:98 `#ifdef MY_APPLICATION`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_dqdsst.h:41 `     &                      FORCES(ng) % dqdt)`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_dqdsst.h:102 `          dqdt(i,j)=fac*Hz(i,j,N(ng))`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:207 `** ANA_SST                 if analytical sea surface temperature, SST        **` | A 판 차이 | 과거 ana_sst는 SST와 열플럭스 민감도를 함께 설정했다. 2013년에 민감도 설정이 ANA_DQDSST 경로로 분리됐다. 인용한 과거 대입식은 사용자 정의가 필요한 템플릿이다. 현재 ANA_SST와 ANA_DQDSST의 포함에는 ANALYTICAL·SOLVE3D·QCORRECTION이 선행한다.<br>git 판정 커밋: `2bc0778d` (2013-10-15). |

| build-options-history:RBH-0451 | ANA_TAIR required | Options.wiki:352 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;ALBEDO&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;BULK_FLUXES&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;ECOSIM&#93;&#93;</code> | ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:246 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:247 `#  if defined ANA_TAIR && \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:248 `    (defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:249 `    (defined ANA_SRFLUX && defined ALBEDO) )`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:250 `#   include <ana_tair.h>`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:125 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:126 `#  if defined ANA_HUMIDITY && \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:127 `    (defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:128 `    (defined ANA_SRFLUX  && defined ALBEDO) )`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:209 `** ANA_TAIR                if analytical surface air temperature             **` | B 문서 오기 | analytical.F의 ANALYTICAL·SOLVE3D 분기에서 ANA_TAIR를 검사한다. 추가 선택지는 BULK_FLUXES, ECOSIM, 또는 ANA_SRFLUX와 ALBEDO의 동시 정의이다. 문서에 열거된 세 옵션을 모두 요구하지 않는다. |

TS_MIX_CLIMA`이다. 이 분기에서 `LtracerCLM(itrc,ng)`가 거짓이면 추적자 자체의 차이를 사용한다. t3dmix.F의 상위 조건은 NONLINEAR·SOLVE3D이며 TS_FIXED는 정의되지 않아야 한다. 인용한 t3dmix2_s.h 선택에는 TS_DIF2와 MIX_S_TS가 필요하다.<br>git 판정 커밋: `b035bf9d` (2016-05-04). |

| build-options-history:RBH-0658 | CONVOLVE 설명 | Options.wiki:777 `:Option to use convolving solution with diffusion operators in the tangent linear, representer and adjoint models.` | roms-3.3:ROMS/Utility/normalization.F:4 `#if defined FOUR_DVAR && defined CONVOLVE`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Utility/normalization.F:40 `!  convolving with the squared-root  adjoint  and  tangent diffusion   !`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Utility/normalization.F:41 `!  operators.                                                          !`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1112 `# ifdef CONVOLVE`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1180 `              CALL ad_convolution (ng, TILE, Lold(ng), Lweak, 2)`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1193 `!  arrays when done with the convolution. Compute representer model`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1194 `!  initial conditions by adding convolved adjoint solution to the`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1195 `!  reference nonlinear state (INIname, record Lbck).`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1207 `              CALL tl_convolution (ng, TILE, Lold(ng), Lweak, 2)`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1213 `              CALL rp_ini_adjust (ng, TILE, Lnew(ng), Lold(ng))`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:491 `#if !defined WEAK_CONSTRAINT && defined FOUR_DVAR`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:492 `# define CONVOLVE`<br>roms-3.3:ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:493 `#endif`<br>`63b208cc 2011-04-22 src:ticket:503`<br>63b208cc:ROMS/Utility/normalization.F:4 `#if defined FOUR_DVAR`<br>63b208cc:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1000 `        CALL error_covariance (iRPM, driver, outer, inner,              &`<br>63b208cc:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1001 `     &                         Lbck, Lini, Lold, Lnew,                  &`<br>63b208cc:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1002 `     &                         Rec1, Rec2, Lposterior)`<br>63b208cc:ROMS/Drivers/w4dvar_ocean.h:1269 `          CALL convolve (driver, Lini, Lold, Lnew)`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/convolve.F:2 `#ifdef FOUR_DVAR`<br>57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/convolve.F:28 `!! | build-options-history:RBH-0661 | COOL_SKIN routine | Options.wiki:792 <code>:'''routine = ''' &#91;&#91;ad_bulk_fluxes.F&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;bulk_fluxes.F&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;rp_bulk_fluxes.F&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;tl_bulk_flux.F&#93;&#93;&lt;section end=COOL_SKIN /&gt;</code> | ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:3 `#ifdef BULK_FLUXES`<br>ROMS/Tangent/tl_bulk_flux.F:3 `#if (defined TANGENT \|\| defined TL_IOMS) && defined BULK_FLUXES_NOT_YET`<br>ROMS/Tangent/tl_bulk_flux.F:330 `# ifdef COOL_SKIN`<br>ROMS/Tangent/tl_bulk_flux.F:2 `      MODULE tl_bulk_flux_mod`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:118 `** COOL_SKIN               if cool skin correction                           **`<br>ROMS/Tangent/tl_bulk_flux.F:331 `      real(r8) :: Clam, Fc, Hcool, Hsb, Hlb, Qbouy, Qcool, lambd`<br>ROMS/Adjoint/ad_bulk_flux.F:1 `#include "cppdefs.h"`<br>ROMS/Adjoint/ad_bulk_flux.F:2 `      MODULE ad_bulk_flux_mod`<br>ROMS/Adjoint/ad_bulk_flux.F:3 `#if defined ADJOINT && defined BULK_FLUXES_NOT_YET`<br>ROMS/Representer/rp_bulk_flux.F:1 `#include "cppdefs.h"`<br>ROMS/Representer/rp_bulk_flux.F:2 `      MODULE rp_bulk_flux_mod`<br>ROMS/Representer/rp_bulk_flux.F:3 `#if defined TL_IOMS && defined BULK_FLUXES_NOT_YET` | B 문서 오기 | 루틴 파일 이름은 ad_bulk_flux.F·bulk_flux.F·rp_bulk_flux.F·tl_bulk_flux.F이다. 수반·접선형·표현자 본문의 선행 조건에는 BULK_FLUXES_NOT_YET가 포함된다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-0680 | DEEPWATER_WAVES conflict | Options.wiki:876 <code>:'''conflict = ''' &#91;&#91;COARE_TAYLOR_YELLAND&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;COARE_OOST&#93;&#93;</code> | ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:818 `# if defined COARE_OOST \|\| defined COARE_TAYLOR_YELLAND \|\|   \`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:819 `     defined DRENNAN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:820 `#  if defined DEEPWATER_WAVES`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:821 `          Cwave(i)=g*MAX(Pwave_top(i,j),eps)*twopi_inv`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:822 `          WaveLength(i)=Cwave(i)*MAX(Pwave_top(i,j),eps)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:823 `#  else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:824 `          Cwave(i)=Lwavep(i,j)/MAX(Pwave_top(i,j),eps)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:825 `          WaveLength(i)=Lwavep(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:826 `#  endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:3 `#ifdef BULK_FLUXES`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:161 `# if defined COARE_TAYLOR_YELLAND \|\| defined DRENNAN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:164 `# if defined COARE_TAYLOR_YELLAND \|\| defined COARE_OOST \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:165 `     defined DRENNAN`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:129 `** DEEPWATER_WAVES         if Deep water waves approximation                 **`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:168 `# if !defined DEEPWATER_WAVES      && \`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:169 `     (defined COARE_TAYLOR_YELLAND \|\| defined COARE_OOST \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:170 `      defined DRENNAN)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:171 `     &                     FORCES(ng) % Lwavep,                         &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:172 `# endif` | B 문서 오기 | BULK_FLUXES 분기에서 COARE_OOST, COARE_TAYLOR_YELLAND, DRENNAN 중 하나가 정의되면 파속과 파장을 계산한다. 그 안의 DEEPWATER_WAVES 분기는 심해파 근사를 선택한다. 문서의 두 COARE 옵션과 DEEPWATER_WAVES는 배타 관계가 아니다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-0705 | DIURNAL_SRFLUX required | Options.wiki:927 <code>:'''required = ''' &#91;&#91;ANA_SRFLUX&#93;&#93;</code> | ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:134 `!  DIURNAL_SRFLUX option: Modulate shortwave radiation SRFLX (which`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:135 `!                         read and interpolated elsewhere) by the local`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:136 `!  diurnal cycle (a function of longitude, latitude and day-of-year).`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:137 `!  This option is provided for cases where SRFLX computed by SET_DATA is`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:138 `!  an average over >= 24 hours. For "diurnal_srflux" to work ana_srflux`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:139 `!  must be undefined. If you want a strictly analytical diurnal cycle`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:140 `!  enter it explicitly at the end of this subroutine or use the "albedo"`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:215 `# if (defined ANA_SRFLUX \|\| defined DIURNAL_SRFLUX) && defined SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:216 `#  include <ana_srflux.h>`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 `#ifdef ANALYTICAL`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:125 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:126 `#  if defined ANA_HUMIDITY && \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:127 `    (defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:128 `    (defined ANA_SRFLUX  && defined ALBEDO) )`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:140 `** DIURNAL_SRFLUX          to impose shortwave radiation local diurnal cycle **`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:177 `# if defined ALBEDO`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:211 `# elif defined DIURNAL_SRFLUX`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:212 `!`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:213 `!  SRFLX is reset on each time step in subroutine SET_DATA which`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:214 `!  interpolates values in the forcing file to the current date.`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:215 `!  This DIURNAL_SRFLUX option is provided so that SRFLX values`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:216 `!  corresponding to a greater or equal daily average can be modulated`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:217 `!  by the local length of day to produce a diurnal cycle with the`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:218 `!  same daily average as the original data.  This approach assumes`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:219 `!  the net effect of clouds is incorporated into the SRFLX data.`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:224 `          IF (ABS(cff1).gt.ABS(cff2)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:225 `            IF (cff1*cff2.gt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:226 `              cff=cff1                                 ! All day case`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:227 `              srflx(i,j)=MAX(0.0_r8,                                    &`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:228 `     &                       srflx(i,j)/cff*                            &`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:229 `     &                       (cff1+cff2*COS(Hangle-lonr(i,j)*deg2rad)))`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:230 `            ELSE`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:231 `              srflx(i,j)=0.0_r8                        ! All night case`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:232 `            END IF`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:233 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:234 `            cff=(cff1*ACOS(-cff1/cff2)+SQRT(cff2*cff2-cff1*cff1))/pi`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:235 `            srflx(i,j)=MAX(0.0_r8,                                      &`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:236 `     &                     srflx(i,j)/cff*                              &`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:237 `     &                     (cff1+cff2*COS(Hangle-lonr(i,j)*deg2rad)))`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:238 `          END IF`<br>ROMS/Functionals/ana_srflux.h:239 `# endif` | B 문서 오기 | 자료에서 읽은 단파 복사에 DIURNAL_SRFLUX의 일변화 조정을 적용할 때 ANA_SRFLUX를 끈다. 이 경로에는 SOLVE3D가 필요하다. ALBEDO가 정의되면 ALBEDO 계산 분기가 먼저 선택된다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-0738 | EAST_FSRADIATION routine | Options.wiki:1014 <code>:'''routine = ''' &#91;&#91;zetabc.F&#93;&#93;&lt;section end=EAST_FSRADIATION /&gt;</code> | roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:231 `# if defined EAST_FSRADIATION`<br>`e8719fb7 2011-09-23 src:ticket:519`<br>e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:231 `        IF (LBC(ieast,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:280 `        IF (LBC(ieast,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN` | A 판 차이 | EAST_FSRADIATION는 과거에 문서가 열거한 루틴에서 사용했다. 현재 해당 루틴은 LBC의 radiation 값을 검사한다. git 이력 판정 근거: [ROMS-git-verify-A2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ROMS-git-verify-A2.md).<br>git 판정 커밋: `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |
| build-options-history:RBH-0739 | EAST_KGRADIENT 설명 | Options.wiki:1017 `:Option to use TKE fields gradient condition for the eastern open boundary condition.` | roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/tkebc_im.F:302 `#  elif defined EAST_KGRADIENT`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/tkebc_im.F:304 `!  Eastern edge, gradient boundary condition.`<br>`e8719fb7 2011-09-23 src:ticket:519`<br>e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/tkebc_im.F:311 `        ELSE IF (LBC(ieast,isMtke,ng)%gradient) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/tkebc_im.F:328 `        ELSE IF (LBC(ieast,isMtke,ng)%gradient) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/tkebc_im.F:3 `#if defined SOLVE3D && (defined MY25_MIXING \|\| defined GLS_MIXING)` | A 판 차이 | EAST_KGRADIENT는 roms-3.5의 동쪽 난류 운동에너지 경계 옵션이다. 현재 선택은 `LBC(ieast,isMtke,ng)%gradient`이다. 이 난류 경계 루틴은 SOLVE3D를 켜고 MY25_MIXING 또는 GLS_MIXING을 켤 때 포함된다. git 이력 판정 근거: [ROMS-git-verify-A2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ROMS-git-verify-A2.md).<br>git 판정 커밋: `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |

| build-options-history:RBH-0759 | EAST_M2NUDGING 설명 | Options.wiki:1062 `:Option to use 2D momentum passive/active nudging term for the eastern open boundary condition.` | roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:328 `#  ifdef EAST_M2NUDGING`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:329 `          IF ((dUdt*dUdx).lt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:330 `            tau=M2obc_in(ng,ieast)`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:331 `          ELSE`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:332 `            tau=M2obc_out(ng,ieast)`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:333 `          END IF`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:334 `          tau=tau*dt2d`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:335 `#  endif`<br>roms-3.5:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:315 `# if defined EAST_M2RADIATION`<br>`e8719fb7 2011-09-23 src:ticket:519`<br>e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:326 `            IF (LBC(ieast,isUbar,ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:508 `              IF (LBC(ieast,isUbar,ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/u2dbc_im.F:496 `        IF (LBC(ieast,isUbar,ng)%radiation) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1802 `            CASE ('RADNUD')`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1803 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%radiation = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1804 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%nudging = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1805 `              S(ib,ifield,igrid)%acquire = .TRUE.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/v2dbc_im.F:1064 `        IF (LBC(ieast,isVbar,ng)%radiation) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/v2dbc_im.F:1076 `              IF (LBC(ieast,isVbar,ng)%nudging) THEN` | A 판 차이 | EAST_M2NUDGING는 roms-3.5의 동쪽 2차원 운동량 경계 옵션이다. 현재 선택은 `LBC(ieast,isUbar,ng)%nudging`와 `LBC(ieast,isVbar,ng)%nudging`이다. nudging은 radiation 분기 안에서 적용한다. git 이력 판정 근거: [ROMS-git-verify-A2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ROMS-git-verify-A2.md).<br>git 판정 커밋: `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |
